<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Başkan gerek gördükçe Parti Meclisini toplantıya çağırabilir. Üye tamsayısının beşte birinin yazılı talebi halinde görevlendirilen genel başkan yardımcısı, gündemi açıklayarak Parti Meclisini toplantıya çağırır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisi üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğu ile toplanır; kanun ve tüzükten kaynaklanan istisnalar dışında mevcudun salt çoğunluğu ile karar verir. Oyların eşitliği halinde, başkanın bulunduğu tarafın dediği olur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisi, bilgi almak ya da bir konuyu görüşmek üzere, toplantılarına partili bakanları, milletvekillerini, belediye ya da il başkanları ile kendi alanında uzman kişileri çağırabilir. Görüşmelere çağrılanlar kurulun üyesi değillerse oy kullanamazlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Geçerli bir özrü olmaksızın olağan toplantılara bir yılda üç kez katılmayanların üyeliği, üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğunca ve gizli oyla alınacak kararla düşer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Görev ve Yetkileri</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 59</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisi, büyük kongreden sonra partinin en yüksek karar organı olmak yetkisi ile;</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">a)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yasa ve tüzük ile büyük kongreye verilmiş yetkiler dışındaki tüm kararları alabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">b)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti programı ve büyük kongre kararları çerçevesinde partinin politikalarını ve stratejisini saptar. Seçim bildirgesini hazırlar. Parti politikalarının halka anlatılıp benimsetilmesi için gerekli çalışmaları yapar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">c)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin hükümet kurmasını, koalisyon ortağı olmasını, hükümetten çekilmesini TBMM grubu başkan vekilleri ile grup yönetim kurulu üyelerinin de görüşünü alarak karara bağlar</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">d)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin kurduğu ya da katıldığı hükümetlerin programı üzerindeki görüşlerini belirtir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">e)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin hükümetle, başka parti ve kuruluşlarla ilişkilerini düzenleme kararları alır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">f)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Büyük Kongreye sunulacak çalışma raporları, öneri ve karar tasarılar hazırlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">g)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin tüzüğünde veya programında yapılacak değişiklik tasarılarını büyük kongreye sunmak üzere hazırlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">h)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Tüzüğün ön gördüğü yönetmelikleri çıkarır. MYK&#8217;nın hazırladığı taslakları da görüşür.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">i)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Seçimlere katılıp katılmamayı, seçimlerde, tüzükte yazılı hükümlere göre uygulanacak aday saptama yöntemini, tüm örgütün görüşünü de alarak, belirleyip karara bağlar. Parti Meclisi bu konudaki yetkilerini başka bir organa devredemez.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">j)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin tüzel kişiliğine son verme veya başka bir parti ile birleşme halinde görüş belirler.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">k)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu Üyelerini seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">l)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yasa ve tüzükte öngörülen diğer görevleri yapar, yetkilerini kullanır ve görev ve yetkilerinin bir kısmını başka organlara devredebilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu (MYK)</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Oluşumu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 60</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu, Parti Meclisi Üyeleri arasından gizli oyla seçilen 20 üye ve Genel Başkandan oluşur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulunun başkanı Genel Başkandır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu, parti amaçlarının gerçekleştirilmesi ve parti işlerinin yürütülmesinde en yüksek yürütme organıdır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">En geç 15 günde bir olağan olarak toplanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulunda boşalma olması halinde, boşalan üyeliklere Parti Meclisi içerisinden bu maddenin 1.fıkra hükümleri çerçevesinde yenileri seçilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Toplanması</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 61</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kuruluna Genel Başkan başkanlık eder. Bulunmadığı durumlarda toplantıyı görevlendireceği bir başkan yardımcısı idare eder.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Başkan ya da görevlendireceği bir Genel başkan yardımcısı gündemi hazırlar ve üyelere duyurur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Başkan gerek gördükçe MYK&#8217;yı toplantıya çağırır. Her toplantıda bir sonraki toplantı günü saptanır. Haklı bir özrü olmaksızın bir yılda 4 toplantıya katılmayanların üyeliği Merkez Yürütme Kurulunun üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğunun gizli oyu ile düşürülebilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Kurul üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğu ile toplanır; kanun ve tüzükten kaynaklanan istisnalar dışında mevcudun salt çoğunluğu ile karar verir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu, bilgi almak için ya da bir konuda görüşmek üzere, toplantılarına TBMM grup başkan vekillerini, partili bakan ve milletvekillerini, belediye ve il başkanları ile gerek gördüğü diğer kişileri çağırabilir. Görüşmeye katılanlar kurul üyesi değilse oy kullanamaz.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Görev ve Yetkileri</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 62</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Yürütme Kurulu, en üst yürütme organı olma yetkileriyle;</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">a)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Kongre ve Parti Meclisinin kararlarını uygular. Parti programını yayar, partinin ilkelerini benimsetmek, seçimleri kazanmak için gerekli tüm girişimleri yapar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">b)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Gerekli il ve ilçelerde örgüt kurulmasına karar verir, Parti örgütünü yönetir, gerekli olması halinde görevden alır, çalışmalarını yönlendirir, örgütle devamlı ilişki sağlar, sorunlarının çözülmesine yardımcı olur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">c)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti örgütünün program, tüzük ve yönetmeliklere uygun şekilde çalışmalarını denetler.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">d)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin hükümetle, başka parti ve kuruluşlarla olan ilişkilerini Parti Meclisinin ilke kararları doğrultusunda düzenler.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">e)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Seçimlerle ilgili olarak Parti Meclisinin aldığı kararları uygular, ilke kararları doğrultusunda Milletvekilliği, belediye başkanlığı, belediye meclis üyeliği ve il genel meclis üyeliği için partiden aday adayı olanlar hakkında tüzüğün verdiği yetkileri kullanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">f)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Araştırma, yayın, parti içi eğitim çalışmalarını düzenler.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">g)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Olağan ve olağanüstü büyük kongrelerle ilgili hazırlıkları yapar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">h)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yıllık bütçeyi hazırlar, parti meclisinin onayından sonra uygular. Büyük kongreye ve ilgili yerlere sunmak üzere kesin hesabı çıkarır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">i)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti gelirlerini arttırmak amacıyla gerekli yasal tedbirleri alır. Gelir makbuzlarını bastırarak gerekli birimlere gönderir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">j)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin amaçlarına uygun olarak Parti Meclisinin onayının alınması koşulu ile taşınmaz mal alımını, satımını veya işletilmesini planlar ve alınan kararları uygular.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">k)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin uluslararası ilişkilerini düzenler, çalışmalarını yürütür.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">l)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Tüzüğün öngördüğü yönetmelikleri hazırlar, Parti Meclisinin onayından sonra uygular.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">m)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Belde, ilçe ve il kongre çalışmalarının, delege seçimlerinin yasa, tüzük ve yönetmeliklerle yetkili kurullarının kararlarına uygun yapılması için çalışır. Denetimlerini düzenli sürdürür ve gerekli kararı gecikmeden alır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">n)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisine sunulacak raporu hazırlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">o)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Tüzükle Merkez Yürütme Kuruluna verilen diğer görevleri yapar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">p)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Dönemsel planlamaları yaparak Parti Meclisinin onayından sonra uygular.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">q)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti danışma konferansını toplar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 36pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-indent: -18pt;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">r)<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; font-family: 'Times New Roman'; font-size: 7pt; line-height: normal; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Danışma Konferansı; Genel Başkan, Parti Meclisi, Merkez Yürütme ve Disiplin Kurulu Üyeleri; Partili Milletvekilleri ve Bakanlar; İl Başkanları, Partili Belediye Başkanları; Merkez Kadın ve Gençlik Meclisleri, Genel Başkan Danışmanları; sivil toplum örgütlerinin partili başkan ya da temsilcileri ve her ilin kendi konferansında her ilin büyük kongre delege sayısı kadar temsilcisinin katılımından oluşur. Merkez Yürütme Kurulu konferans bileşimini genişletebilir. Parti Danışma Konferansı yılda en az bir kere ve ayrıca Genel Başkan veya MYK&#8217;nın uygun göreceği tarihlerde toplanır. Parti Danışma Konferansları; Büyük kongreye siyasal kararlar ve seçimlerle ilgili tavsiyelerde bulunur ve Parti Meclisi ile Merkez Yürütme Kurulunun yetki ve görev alanına giren konularda görüşlerini bildirir. Parti Konferanslarının takvimini Merkez Yürütme Kurulu belirler ve İl Örgütleri ile gerekli kurum, kişi ve birimlere bildirir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Başkanlık Kurulu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 63</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Başkanlık Kurulu, Genel Başkan, Genel Başkan Yardımcıları ve Meclis Grup Başkan Vekillerinden oluşur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisinin kararlarının pratik uygulamasını gözlemler. MYK&#8217;nın toplanmasının zor olduğu ya da zaman alacağı durumlarda inisiyatif alır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Sayman</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 64</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Sayman, Merkez Yürütme Kurulunca kendi üyeleri arasından, üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğunun gizli oyu ile seçilir. Adaylardan birinin tek başına salt çoğunluğun oyunu alamaması halinde, en çok oy alan iki aday arasında yapılacak ikinci tur oylamada en çok oy alan aday seçilir. Merkez Yürütme Kurulunun salt çoğunluğunun gizli oyu ile görevden alınabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel sayman, Genel Başkanın onayıyla Merkez Yürütme Kurulu üyeleri arasından bir yardımcı seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel Sayman parti gelirlerinin toplanması, harcamaların yapılması, gelir gider hesapları konusunda yerel örgütlerin denetlenmesi; gelir-gider defterinin, bütçe ve kesin hesapların yasalar ve tüzüğe uygun bir biçimde tutulmasının sağlanması ve yetkili kurulların bu konulara ilişkin kararlarının uygulanması ile görevlidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Kurullarda Görev Bölümü</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 65</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisi, Merkez Yürütme Kurulu ve Genel başkan yardımcıları ile genel başkan danışmanlarının çalışma yöntemleri ve görev bölümleri ile merkezde kurulacak büroların kuruluş, işleyiş biçimleri ve kadroları yönetmelikle düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yargı İsteği İle Kongre</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 66</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Büyük Kongre veya il ve ilçe kongrelerinde yapılan seçimlerin, ilgili seçim kurulu başkanlığınca iptal edilmesi durumunda, Seçim Kurulu Başkanının saptadığı ve bildirdiği gün, saat ve yerde seçimlerin yenilenmesi için kongre toplanır. Bu kongrede yalnız seçim yapılır, görevini yapmayarak veya savsaklayarak seçimlerin iptaline neden olan sorumlular hakkında disiplin işlemi uygulanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Disiplin Kurulu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 67</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Disiplin Kurulu, Büyük Kongrece seçilen 7 asil ve 4 yedek üyeden oluşur. Kurul seçimden sonraki 15 gün içinde yapacağı ilk toplantıda, başkan ve sekreter üyeyi seçer. Çalışma koşul ve yöntemleri, tüzüğün &#8220;Disiplin ve Denetim&#8221; bölümündeki hükümlerle düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">DÖRDÜNCÜ KISIM; MERKEZ KADIN VE GENÇLİK MECLİSLERİ</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Kadın Meclisi</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 68</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez kadın meclisi, özgün ve özerk örgütlenir, tüzüğün ilgili bölümünde sıralandığı gibi belde, ilçe, il ve genel merkez örgütleri olmak üzere merkezi yapılanmaya sahiptir. Köy ve mahalle birim ve temsilcileri de oluşturabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Çalışmalarını sürdürmek üzere Genel Merkez, il, ilçe ve beldelerde ayrı bina tutulabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Kadın Meclisi merkezi düzeyde Parti Meclisine karşı sorumludur. İl kadın meclisi, il yönetimine karşı; ilçe kadın meclisi ilçe yönetimine karşı sorumludur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Her kademedeki kadın kuruluşu, işleyişi, örgütlenmesi; belde, ilçe, il ve büyük kongreleri; yönetim kurulları, kurulların çalışma biçim ve düzeni, atanma ve görevden alınmaları, bina tutmaları ile genel örgüt yapısı ile karşılıklı ilişki ve konumu Parti Meclisince çıkarılacak &#8220;Kadın Meclisi Yönetmeliğinde&#8221; düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-left: 18pt; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez Gençlik Meclisi</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 69</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkez gençlik meclisi, özgün ve özerk örgütlenir. Adı Demokratik Yurtsever Gençlik, kısa adı DYG&#8217;dir. Meclisin kendine ait flama ve sembolleri olabilir. Merkez gençlik meclisi, tüzüğün ilgili bölümünde sıralandığı gibi belde, ilçe, il ve Genel Merkez örgütleri olmak üzere merkezi yapılanmaya sahiptir. Köy ve mahalle birim temsilcileri de oluşturabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Çalışmalarını sürdürmek üzere Genel Merkez, il, ilçe ve beldelerde ayrı bina tutabilirler.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Her kademedeki Gençlik kuruluşu, işleyişi, örgütlenmesi; belde, ilçe ve il kongreleri; yönetim kurulları, kurulların çalışma biçim ve düzeni, atanma ve görevden alınmaları, bina tutmaları ile genel örgüt yapısı ile karşılıklı ilişki ve konumu çıkarılacak &#8220;Gençlik Meclisi Yönetmeliğinde&#8221; düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">BEŞİNCİ KISIM; PARTİ GRUPLARI</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">TBMM Parti Grubu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">T.B.M.M. de Grup Kurulması</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 70</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partili milletvekilleri, partinin TBMM Grubunu oluştururlar. Yasada belirtilen yeter sayıda milletvekili varsa grup kurulduğu, parti Genel Başkanı tarafından TBMM Başkanlığına yazı ile bildirilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grubun çalışmaları iç yönetmeliğe göre düzenlenir. Siyasi Partiler Yasasının 23. maddesindeki ilkelere uygun olarak hazırlanıp kabul edilen Grup İç Yönetmeliği, grubun kurulduğunun bildirildiği tarihten itibaren 15 gün içinde TBMM başkanlığına sunulur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup iç yönetmeliğinde partinin tüzük ve programına aykırı hükümler yer almaz.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup Genel Kurulu partili tüm milletvekillerinden oluşur. Bakanlar Kuruluna ya da bir bakana güven ya da güvensizlik oyu verilmesi için karar alma yetkisi, Grup Genel Kurulunundur. Bu yetki bir başka organa devredilemez. Grup Genel Kurulunda, seçimlerle ilgili oylamalar ile üyelere ilişkin bağlayıcı nitelikteki oylamalar gizli oyla yapılır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup Başkanı</span></b></div>
