<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 71</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin Genel Başkanı bir milletvekili ise parti grubu ve grup yönetim kurulunun da başkanıdır; değilse parti grubu üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğu ile grup üyelerinden birisini grup başkanı seçer. İlk iki turda salt çoğunluk sağlanamadığı takdirde, üçüncü oylamada en çok oy alan seçilmiş olur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Grubu Yönetim Kurulu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 72</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">TBMM Grubu yönetim kurulu; grup başkanı, grup genel kurulunun kendi üyeleri arasından seçtiği iki başkan vekili ve yeteri kadar asil ve yedek üyeden oluşur.<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;</span></span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup organları her toplantı yılının başında ve o toplantı yılı için seçilir. Seçimler gizli oyla yapılır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup adına konuşmaya ve beyanatta bulunmaya grup başkanı yetkilidir. Bulunmadığı zamanlarda grup başkan vekilleri ya da ivedi durumlarda seçilen sözcüler bu yetkiyi kullanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yasama girişimlerinin düzenli bir biçimde yürütülmesi ve meclis çalışmalarında uyum ve disiplin sağlanması grup yönetim kurulunun görevidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup Kurulamayan Durumlar</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 73</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partili milletvekillilerin sayısı grup kurmaya yetmiyorsa, bu milletvekilleri partinin genel tutumunu ilgilendiren ya da Genel Başkanca gerekli görülen konularda toplanarak ortak tutum belirler. Aralarındaki işbirliğinin ve görev bölümünün kurallarını Genel Başkana danışarak saptarlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">T.B.M.M Grup Disiplin Kurulu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 74</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup disiplin kurulu, grup genel kurulunca gizli oyla ihtiyaç duyulan miktarda seçilen asil ve yedek üyeden oluşur. Seçimden sonraki on beş gün içinde yapılacak ilk toplantıda grup disiplin kurulu, başkan ve sekreter üyeyi seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup disiplin kurulunun çalışma düzeni, yöntemleri ve yetkileri tüzüğün &#8220;Disiplin ve Denetim&#8221; bölümündeki hükümlere göre düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup Üyelerinin Görev ve Sorumlulukları</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 75</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partili Milletvekilleri, yasama çalışmalarında, partinin program, tüzük, büyük kongre ve Parti Meclisi kararları doğrultusunda çalışmak, grup genel kurulu ve grup yönetim kurulu kararlarını uygulamak ve desteklemekle yükümlüdürler. Geçerli özürleri olmadıkça Meclis Genel Kuruluna, komisyon ve grup toplantılarına katılırlar. Bu toplantılara özürsüz üç kez üst üste gelmeyen üyeye grup yönetim kurulunca uyarı yapılır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">İl Genel Meclisi ve Belediye Meclisi Grupları</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 76</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Belediyelerde; belediye başkanı partili ise, partinin meclis grubunun da doğal başkanıdır. Belediye başkanı partili değilse belediye meclisi parti grubu kendi içinde grup başkanını, grup başkan vekilini ve grup sözcüsünü seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Belediye başkanı, yardımcılarını kendisi seçer. Aylık haklarını belediye meclisi düzenler. Başkan izne veya şehir dışına çıktığı zaman başkan vekilini kendisi seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">İl genel meclisinde çoğunluğun partimizde olduğu yerlerde ilk grup toplantısında il genel meclisi başkanlığı için aday tespiti yapılır. İl genel meclisi üyeleri kendi aralarında gizli oyla ve grubun salt çoğunluğuyla adayı seçer. Salt çoğunluk sağlanamaması halinde en çok oy aylan iki adayın katıldığı ikinci oylamada en fazla oyu alan aday, il genel meclisi başkanlığına grup adayı olarak seçilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bütün il genel meclislerinde ayrıca grup başkanı seçilir. Seçim yukarıdaki yöntemle olur. Bu grup kendi içinden başkan vekillerini ve sözcüsünü de seçer.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">İl, ilçe ve belde başkanları parti gruplarını &#8220;Bütçe&#8221; , &#8216;&#8217;Yıllık Program&#8221; ve &#8220;Faaliyet Raporu &#8220; görüşmeleri öncesi olmak üzere bir yıl içinde en az üç defa toplantıya çağırır. Ayrıca ihtiyaç duyulduğu kadar toplantı yapılabilir. Bu toplantılar bir periyoda bağlanabileceği gibi, olağanüstü toplantılar halinde de yapılabilir. Parti örgütü ile ortak yapılan grup toplantılarına ilgili parti kademesi başkanı başkanlık eder.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Belediye başkanlarının yürütme ile ilgili yasal görevleri konusunda bağlayıcı grup kararı alınamaz.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti grupları il, ilçe ve belde başkan ve yönetim kurullarının görüş ve önerilerini dikkate alır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Grup üyeleri, partinin program, tüzük ve ilkeleri ile büyük kongre, kendi kongreleri ve gruplarının kararlarına uymakla yükümlüdürler. Gruplarda alınan bağlayıcı kararlara aykırı davranış, ihraç sebebidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Belediye ve il genel meclisi parti gruplarının oluşturulması, çalışma yöntemleri ve bu grupların parti örgütü ile ilişkileri &#8220;Yerel Yönetimlerle İlişkiler Yönetmeliğinde&#8217;&#8217; düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">ALTINCI KISIM</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkezi Danışma Büroları</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Oluşumu</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 77</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">MYK&#8217;nın teklifi ve PM&#8217;nin onayıyla aşağıda belirtilen konularda MYK&#8217;ya bağlı olarak araştırma yapmak, bu konularda oluşacak parti politikalarına katkıda bulunmak üzere, Merkezi Danışma Büroları oluşturulur.<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;</span></span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bileşimi</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 78</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bürolar PM tarafından, her büro bir MYK üyesinin sorumluluğunda, konusuyla ilgili parti üyesi ve ya üye olmayan sayısı PM tarafından belirlenecek uzmanlardan oluşur.<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;</span></span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bürolar</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 79</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">1) AB- Müzakere Sürecini İzleme</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">2) İş ve Çalışma Yaşamı</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">3) Tarımsal Politikalar ve Tarım Üreticilerinin Sorunları<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">4) Siyasi İşler</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">5) Ekonomi ve Ekonomi Politikaları bürosu olmak üzere 5 bürodan oluşur. PM Kararıyla bu sayı arttırılıp azaltılabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bürolara ilişkin ortak hükümler</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 80</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Merkezi Danışma Büroları MYK&#8217;ya bağlı olarak, kendi konularında bilimsel araştırmalar yapar, bu konularda parti politikalarının oluşumuna katkıda bulunacak toplantı-konferans-sempozyum vb. çeşitli akademik aktivitelerde bulunarak, sonuçlarını MYK&#8217;ya aktarır. Büroların çalışma yöntemi, çıkarılacak yönetmelikle düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">DÖRDÜNCÜ BÖLÜM</span></b></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">PARTİ EĞİTİM OKULLARI VE AKADEMİLERİ</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partide Eğitim</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 81</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin en temel hedeflerinden biri de; hak ve özgürlüklerinin derin bilinci ile hareket eden, değişim ve dönüşümü toplumsal yaşamın her alanında yaratacağı örgütlülükle sağlamayı hedefleyen aktif, birikimli, çağdaş ve demokrasiyi bir yaşam biçimi ve kültür olarak içselleştiren üyeler yaratarak toplumun değişimine katkı sunmaktır. Bu bakımdan bireyi ve toplumu değiştirmenin başlıca aracı olan eğitimi, parti içinde yoğun, yaygın ve etkin bir biçimde yürütmek en temel çalışma alanlarından biridir. Eğitim, partimizin temel bir işlevi olduğundan hiç bir şekilde yürütülen farklı faaliyetlerin gölgesinde ikincil bir çalışma olarak ele alınamaz.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin eğitim programlarına katılma her üye ve yöneticinin en temel hak ve görevidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Eğitim faaliyetleri, bu alanda görevlendirilen bir genel başkan yardımcısının başkanlığında oluşturulan Merkezi Eğitim Kurulunca organize edilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Eğitim genel merkezde verilebileceği gibi; il, ilçe ve belde örgütleri düzeyinde ya da panel, seminer, konferans gibi etkinliklerle zenginleştirilerek de verilebilir. Aktif eğitim temel bir yöntem olarak uygulanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Toplu eğitimin yanında yaşamın her anında üyelerin bireysel eğitimleriyle ilgili aktiviteler içinde olmaları esastır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Hedeflenen eğitimin; siyaset, yerel, kadın ve gençlik alanlarında akademi düzeyinde yapılması esas alınır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bu nedenle Parti Meclisi kararıyla eğitim; Siyaset, Yerel Yönetimler, Kadın ve Gençlik Akademileri olarak altyapısı hazırlanır ve programlanarak kurulur.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti içi eğitim, eğitimin organizasyonu, çıkarılacak müfredatın niteliği, okul ve akademilerdeki eğitimde görevlendirilecek kadro ve eğitmenlerin görev ve yetkileri Parti Meclisince çıkarılacak Eğitim Yönetmeliğinde düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">YURT DIŞI TEMSİLCİLİKLERİ</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 82</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partimiz, yurt dışında yurttaşlarımızın yoğun olarak yaşadığı ülkelerle diğer yerlerde Parti Meclisinin kararı ile yurt dışı temsilcilikleri açabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Kurulacak temsilciliklerin, tüzükte gösterilmesi zorunluluğu nedeniyle, aşağıdaki ülke ve şehirler belirlenmiştir;</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Paris<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(Fransa)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Berlin, Hamburg, Köln, Münih, Düseldorf&nbsp;<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(Almanya)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Stockholm, Upsala,&nbsp;<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(İsveç)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Amsterdam<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(Hollanda)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Bürüksel<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(Belçika)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Londra<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(İngiltere)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Atina<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>(Yunanistan)</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">

<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Adaylar ve Sıralarının Tespiti Usulleri</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 84</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Milletvekili genel ve ara seçimlerinde aday adaylıkları Parti Meclisince uygun bulunanlar arasından adayların sırasının tespiti;</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">a) Ön seçim,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">b) Aday Yoklaması,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">c) Merkez Yoklaması,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Usul ve esaslarından biri veya bir kaçı ile yapılabilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yurdun tamamında bu usullerden yalnızca birinin ya da her seçim çevresine göre bu usullerden bir ya da bir kaçının uygulanacağına Parti Meclisi karar verir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">a) Ön Seçim: Seçim çevresindeki bütün parti üyelerinin katılarak Yargı gözetiminde yapılacak seçimlerle parti adaylarının ve sıralamalarının belirlenmesidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Ön seçimler, Siyasi Partiler Yasasının ilgili hükümleri, tüzük ve çıkarılacak yönetmelikte belirtilen esaslara göre yapılır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">b) Aday Yoklaması: Aday yoklaması aşağıda belirtilen partililerin katılımıyla yapılan milletvekili aday tespit yöntemidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Aday yoklaması seçimlerine katılacak partililer şunlardır:</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Seçim çevresinde oturan ve üyelikleri devam eden; parti kurucuları, eski bakan ve milletvekilleri,<span style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>eski il ve belediye başkanları,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Seçim çevresinin bakan ve milletvekilleri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">İl, ilçe, belde başkanları ve yönetim kurulu üyeleri ile il disiplin kurulu üyeleri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">İl, İlçe İl Kadın ve gençlik meclisleri temsilcileri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partili belediye başkanları, belediye ve il genel meclisi üyeleri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partili köy ve mahalle muhtarları,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Büyük kongre, il kongresi ve ilçe kongresi delegeleri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Köy ve mahalle komisyonları ile diğer sürekli komisyon temsilcileri ve grupların üyeleri,</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Sivil toplum örgütlerinin partili başkan ya da temsilcileri.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">c) Merkez Yoklaması: Tüm ülkede ya da belirlenecek bazı seçim çevrelerinde milletvekili adayları ve sıralarının Parti Meclisince belirlenmesidir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Ön seçim sonuçlarının yetkili kurullarca geçersiz sayılması halinde adaylar ve sıraları merkez yoklaması yöntemiyle belirlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Ön seçimlerde eşit sayıda oy almış adayların öncelik sırası, tüzüğün 4. maddesi (e) fıkrasının 2. cümlesinde belirtilen esasa göre belirlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Meclisi; ön seçim ya da aday yoklaması yapılmasına karar verilen seçim çevrelerinde, toplam olarak TBMM üye tam sayısının % 5&#8217;ini aşmamak üzere, ilini, seçim çevresini, aday listesindeki sırasını ön seçim ya da aday yoklaması tarihinden en az 10 gün önce Yüksek Seçim Kuruluna bildirmek koşuluyla merkez adayı gösterebilir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yerel Seçimlerde Parti Adaylarının Belirlenmesi</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 85</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Yerel Seçimlerde parti adaylarının belirlenmesi bu bölümdeki kurallar ve ilgili yasalar göz önünde tutularak hazırlanacak yönetmelikte düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Parti Adaylarının Bildirilmesi</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 86</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Genel</span><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">&nbsp;Başkan, Parti Meclisince saptanan ve aday yoklaması suretiyle tespit edilen adayları ön seçimle belirlenen aday listelerine katarak seçim çevrelerine göre düzenler ve Yüksek Seçim Kuruluna süresinde verilmesini sağlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Eksikliklerin Tamamlanması</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 87</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Aday listelerinin kesinleşmeden önce herhangi bir nedenle bu listelerde boşalma olursa ve listedeki sıraya göre kaydırma sonunda noksanlıklar doldurulamazsa eksiklikler Parti Meclisince tamamlanır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Adaylık Yönetmeliği</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 88</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Milletvekilliği seçimlerinde ve mahalli seçimlerde parti adaylığına başvurma, aday adayları ile ilgili olarak yapılacak incelemenin niteliği ve yöntemi, adaylığa başvuranlardan ve adaylardan alınacak ödentinin ölçüsünü, adayların belirlenmesinde uygulanacak yöntem ve adaylıkla ilgili diğer konular &#8220;Adaylık Yönetmeliğinde&#8221; düzenlenir.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">ALTINCI BÖLÜM</span></div>
<div align="center" class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: center;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">HÜKÜMET KURMA KATILMA VE HÜKMETTEN AYRILMA</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Görev ve Yetki</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b style="background-color: transparent; border: 0px; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;"><span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Madde 89</span></b></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Hükümet kurulmasına, bir hükümete katılmaya ya da partinin hükümetten ayrılmasına Parti Meclisi karar verir. Partinin TBMM grubu varsa, bu yetki kullanımına grup başkan vekilleri ve grup yönetim kurulu üyeleri de katılır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin kurduğu hükümette bakanları, Merkez Yürütme Kurulu ve TBMM grup yönetim kurlunun görüşünü alarak partili başbakan belirler. Genel Başkan başbakan değilse, başbakan ile Genel Başkan, bakanların seçimini birlikte yaparlar.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: transparent; background-position: initial initial; background-repeat: initial initial; border: 0px; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">Partinin katıldığı hükümete verilecek bakanlar konusunda başbakanın muhatabı Genel Başkandır.</span></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoBodyText" style="background-color: white; border: 0px; color: #444444; font-family: 'Lucida Grande', 'Lucida Sans Unicode', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20.390625px; margin-bottom: 15px; outline: 0px; padding: 0px;">
